.. _api-library-geometry-and-appearance:

Geometry and appearance
=======================

Geometry, colour and font value types, theme recipes, the theme engine's primitives and deterministic kernels, surface materials and connected-control geometry.

Types
-----

.. list-table::
   :class: autosummary longtable
   :widths: 35 65

   * - :cpp:class:`AmbientEffect <gui_forms::AmbientEffect>`
     - An ambient effect (E7): its part repaints as time passes, at most ``frames_per_second``, and reads time_in_state within one ``period`` (it wraps), so that the effect cycles.
   * - :cpp:class:`AncestorBloom <gui_forms::AncestorBloom>`
     - The keys an ancestor chain holds, hashed into 256 bits: a rule whose ancestor keys are not all present cannot match, and is rejected without walking the chain (CSS engines' ancestor Bloom filter).
   * - :cpp:class:`AncestorFacts <gui_forms::AncestorFacts>`
     - One ancestor control as a selector's ancestor compounds read it: borrowed from the control for the resolution.
   * - :cpp:class:`ApplicationColourRole <gui_forms::ApplicationColourRole>`
     - An application role as the theme compiled it: its name, its fallback built-in role, whether it is a pair (its fallback's shape), and its declarations in sheet order from its last unconditional one (the base colours, where there is one), each later one holding where its condition does.
   * - :cpp:class:`ApplicationColourRoleVariant <gui_forms::ApplicationColourRoleVariant>`
     - One declaration of an application role's colours: its foreground and, for a pair, its background, where ``media`` holds.
   * - :cpp:class:`AssembledPartMaterial <gui_forms::AssembledPartMaterial>`
     - A part's assembled material (T3 EC1): its recipe's material, the floor, with the winning material fields of the theme's rules in place, and what it was assembled from.
   * - :cpp:class:`AuthoredGradient <gui_forms::AuthoredGradient>`
     - A gradient as written: - ``kind``: linear_gradient, radial_gradient or conic_gradient; - ``repeating``: CSS's repeating- form; - ``angle_degrees``: a linear gradient's CSS angle (0 up, 90 right), unless ``corner`` names one, or a conic gradient's ``from``; - ``centre_x``, ``centre_y``: a radial or conic gradient's centre, across and down the box; - ``circle``, ``extent``, ``radius_x``, ``radius_y``: a radial gradient's shape and size; with ``lengths``, an ellipse's radii, each along its own axis, or a circle's radius, radius_x's units; - ``stops``: at least one, at most maximum_authored_gradient_stops.
   * - :cpp:class:`AuthoredGradientStop <gui_forms::AuthoredGradientStop>`
     - One colour stop.
   * - :cpp:class:`BackdropDeclaration <gui_forms::BackdropDeclaration>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`BackdropStep <gui_forms::BackdropStep>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`BackplaneRegion <gui_forms::BackplaneRegion>`
     - One backplane image's region (E9-B): ``extent`` device pixels at ``scale``, its top-left pixel at ``origin`` in the window's device pixels (the window's logical origin times the scale; possibly fractional).
   * - :cpp:class:`BandDeclaration <gui_forms::BandDeclaration>`
     - A ``window`` rule's band (owner, 2026-11-08: the window outer band): ``band-width`` (logical units, before device-pixel snapping), ``band-color`` (the rest colour), ``band-lit-color`` and ``band-effect``.
   * - :cpp:class:`BasicControlStyle <gui_forms::BasicControlStyle>`
     - BasicControlStyle retains the compact compatibility palette used by callers that do not select full role recipes.
   * - :cpp:class:`BoundedRange <gui_forms::BoundedRange>`
     - At most Capacity passes: ``count`` clamped to Capacity.
   * - :cpp:class:`BoundedRange::Iterator <gui_forms::BoundedRange::Iterator>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`BoxLength <gui_forms::BoxLength>`
     - A length along one axis of the part's box: ``fraction`` of the box's extent on that axis plus ``units`` logical units, as CSS's <length-percentage> (a percentage, a length, or the calc() of both).
   * - :cpp:class:`BundledFontFamily <gui_forms::BundledFontFamily>`
     - A bundled font family a theme names in place of a role's faces (theme-language ``font-family: Selawik``).
   * - :cpp:class:`CachedPartRaster <gui_forms::CachedPartRaster>`
     - A part's cached raster and what drew it: the primitive, every parameter (animated ones included, since they change the pixels), the target with its states restricted to those the primitive reads, and the extent.
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasArea <gui_forms::CanvasArea>`
     - A filled and outlined canvas part (the background, a node, its header, a port, a lamp, the marquee): its area, its line, the line's width and its corner radius, in view units.
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasFlow <gui_forms::CanvasFlow>`
     - What moves along a flowing link (the link part at rest): the mark, its speed in world units a second and its pitch in view units.
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasGrid <gui_forms::CanvasGrid>`
     - The world grid: minor and major colours, one line width (view units), the minor spacing in world units at zoom 1, and the band of view spacings the shown level keeps to (select_grid_level; its maximum is exactly grid_level_ratio times its minimum).
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasItem <gui_forms::CanvasItem>`
     - One item of the canvas (a node, a port or a link) as theme rules address it with ``:item()``: its stable ID (``:item(#n42)``) and classes (``:item(.warning)``).
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasLine <gui_forms::CanvasLine>`
     - A link's stroke: its colour and width, its dash and gap (a dash of 0 is solid), and its halo, a wider stroke ``halo_width`` beyond each side drawn under it (0 draws none).
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasLook <gui_forms::CanvasLook>`
     - Every canvas part in every state, as one theme draws them in one colour scheme and contrast.
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasTextureLayer <gui_forms::CanvasTextureLayer>`
     - One layer of the node canvas's texture (``background-texture``; cp31), composited under the grid at ``contrast``, a fraction in [0, maximum_canvas_texture_contrast]: - ``procedural``: a built-in pattern (ProceduralFill's ``pattern``, ``seed``, ``tile`` in [minimum_procedural_tile, maximum_canvas_texture_tile] logical units and ``strength`` in [0, 1]); the canvas chooses its colours (resolve_canvas_look), so a theme sets its form only; - ``finish``: a surface finish (``finish``, any kind but the glint, in the colours the theme writes); the pattern fields are not read.
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasTextureLayers <gui_forms::CanvasTextureLayers>`
     - The texture's layers, bottom first: capacity maximum_canvas_texture_layers, ``count`` live (0 is ``none``).
   * - :cpp:class:`CanvasView <gui_forms::CanvasView>`
     - Where the canvas lies in the painter's coordinates: ``origin`` is world (0, 0) and ``zoom`` view units per world unit (finite and positive; the background draws no grid otherwise).
   * - :cpp:class:`CheckerPattern <gui_forms::CheckerPattern>`
     - An alpha checkerboard (piece C7): square cells ``cell`` units wide, alternating ``first`` and ``second``, with the corner of a ``first`` cell at ``anchor``, so the pattern stays put while the filled rectangle moves.
   * - :cpp:class:`Color <gui_forms::Color>`
     - gui_forms::Color is an explicit unpremultiplied RGBA value with opaque alpha default.
   * - :cpp:class:`ColourOrigin <gui_forms::ColourOrigin>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules>`
     - A sheet compiled against a registry: the rules that survived, each declaration checked, in the order the cascade visits them.
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::CompiledPresentationState <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::CompiledPresentationState>`
     - One state of a compiled machine (see PresentationState).
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::CompiledTransition <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::CompiledTransition>`
     - A checked transition: an engine property, or the parameter at ``parameter``.
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::FieldProvenance <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::FieldProvenance>`
     - Where one resolved field came from (T4 cascade spec, item 5).
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::PartFacts <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::PartFacts>`
     - What a part is: the facts its selectors are matched against.
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::PartProvenance <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::PartProvenance>`
     - The provenance of every field of the part ``facts`` name: its primitive parameters (by position; ``parameter_count`` are the chosen primitive's, none when no rule selects one), its material fields and its recipe fields (by their positions), as resolve(), resolve_recipe_fields() and the primitive's parameters resolve them.
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::RecipeResolution <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::RecipeResolution>`
     - The winning declaration of each recipe field (RecipeField) of the rules matching a control, by the field's position, or null where no matching rule sets it; they belong to the compiled rules.
   * - :cpp:class:`CompiledThemeRules::Resolution <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::Resolution>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ConnectedControlSeam <gui_forms::ConnectedControlSeam>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ConnectedControlTopology <gui_forms::ConnectedControlTopology>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ConnectedControlVisualGeometry <gui_forms::ConnectedControlVisualGeometry>`
     - The resolved recipe owns no renderer object.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlCanvasPaint <gui_forms::ControlCanvasPaint>`
     - A node canvas part's paint as a theme sets it (RecipeField::fill_colour to flow_spacing, and RecipeField::stroke_width as ``stroke_width``; cp31), each unset where no rule sets it: resolve_canvas_look (gui_forms/theme/canvas_look) then takes the stock value it states for the part.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlPartMetrics <gui_forms::ControlPartMetrics>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlRoleRecipes <gui_forms::ControlRoleRecipes>`
     - ControlRoleRecipes stores complete ordinary, selected, high-contrast, and high-contrast-selected recipes across the closed surface-state axis.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlRoleTable <gui_forms::ControlRoleTable>`
     - The recipes of every control visual role, indexed by ControlVisualRole, held out of line.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlStateRecipes <gui_forms::ControlStateRecipes>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlStripePattern <gui_forms::ControlStripePattern>`
     - A part's metrics and shape as a theme sets them (RecipeField::block_size to edge_shadow_colour), each unset where no rule sets it: the control then keeps its own metric and the part its rectangle.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlTextShadow <gui_forms::ControlTextShadow>`
     - A text shadow (E9.6, CSS ``text-shadow``): the text's glyphs, moved by ``offset`` logical units (each axis within ±maximum_text_shadow_offset), blurred by ``blur_radius`` (the shadows' blur-radius convention, 0 to maximum_text_shadow_blur; both limits are the theme rules') and drawn in ``color`` under the glyphs.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlVisualContext <gui_forms::ControlVisualContext>`
     - ControlVisualContext selects a role recipe from surface state plus selected, focused, defaulted, and high-contrast axes.
   * - :cpp:class:`ControlVisualRecipe <gui_forms::ControlVisualRecipe>`
     - ControlVisualRecipe combines a layered material with text/glyph colors, focus/default rings, and pressed content displacement.
   * - :cpp:class:`CursorImage <gui_forms::CursorImage>`
     - Top-down, straight-alpha sRGB.
   * - :cpp:class:`CursorImages <gui_forms::CursorImages>`
     - Immutable owned representations of one logical cursor.
   * - :cpp:class:`DamageRegion <gui_forms::DamageRegion>`
     - DamageRegion incrementally merges exact rectangular unions, preserves disjoint damage up to 64 rectangles, then collapses to one bound with explicit compaction/collapse telemetry.
   * - :cpp:class:`DeviceExtent <gui_forms::DeviceExtent>`
     - A raster's size in device pixels.
   * - :cpp:class:`DeviceScale <gui_forms::DeviceScale>`
     - Device pixels per logical unit.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectAdvance <gui_forms::EffectAdvance>`
     - What advancing an instance's time did.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectArray <gui_forms::EffectArray>`
     - A fixed-capacity array with a live count: ``items`` holds Capacity values, the first ``count`` of them live.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectCanvas <gui_forms::EffectCanvas>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectCanvasMeter <gui_forms::EffectCanvasMeter>`
     - What one frame's canvas did: device pixels charged against the budget, and the calls refused for the budget or the save depth.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectColourDeclaration <gui_forms::EffectColourDeclaration>`
     - One effect colour as the rules resolve it: the role's colour in the part's palette and the role it came from.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectColours <gui_forms::EffectColours>`
     - The colours an effect draws with (EP-4): its part's primary and secondary colour roles as the host control resolves them from its theme and colour scheme (effect_primary_property, effect_secondary_property), or the host's own colours where the theme names none.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectCost <gui_forms::EffectCost>`
     - An effect's per-frame budget: ``fixed`` operations (proved by the plugin's static bound) plus ``per_device_pixel`` operations for each device pixel of the part and its outsets (metered by the canvas, which refuses drawing past it; owner decision 1(a)).
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectDeclaration <gui_forms::EffectDeclaration>`
     - What an effect is, as its class declares it in ``static constexpr EffectDeclaration declaration``.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectEntry <gui_forms::EffectEntry>`
     - One registered effect type: its declaration, its parent's name, its state layout and the thunks the engine calls.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectFrame <gui_forms::EffectFrame>`
     - What one frame of ``run`` is given.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectGradient <gui_forms::EffectGradient>`
     - A gradient's stops: the first ``count`` of ``stops`` are live, offsets in [0, 1] and non-decreasing.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectInputEvent <gui_forms::EffectInputEvent>`
     - One granted input event: the pointer in part-local logical units, or a key's code (KeyCode's value).
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectInstance <gui_forms::EffectInstance>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectInstanceCounters <gui_forms::EffectInstanceCounters>`
     - What an instance has counted over its life.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectLoadDiagnostics <gui_forms::EffectLoadDiagnostics>`
     - What effect registration found in this process, for diagnostics and the windows' metrics (MetricsSnapshot::effects_unverified): effects registered, and those registered unverified.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectPointer <gui_forms::EffectPointer>`
     - The pointer's standing state over the part at the frame's start (design note section 8a(1)): whether it is inside the part, whether a button is held, and where it was last seen while inside, in part-local logical units.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectRandom <gui_forms::EffectRandom>`
     - The engine's deterministic generator (owner amendment, 2026-11-02): a counter-based hash of (seed, stream, counter), SplitMix64's finalizer over their mix, with no host randomness and no hidden state beyond the counter in this value.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectRegistry <gui_forms::EffectRegistry>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectSelection <gui_forms::EffectSelection>`
     - What a control's effect selection came to, kept by the control for diagnostics: the name asked for (a copy, empty for none), who asked and the verdict.
   * - :cpp:class:`EffectSetup <gui_forms::EffectSetup>`
     - What ``setup`` is given: once when the effect is instantiated on a part, and again on a geometry change if the effect declares setup_on_geometry.
   * - :cpp:class:`EngineExpression <gui_forms::EngineExpression>`
     - A compiled program: immutable, a fixed-size value with no allocation, so it can be copied into a rule table and evaluated from any number of parts on the UI thread.
   * - :cpp:class:`EnginePartRecord <gui_forms::EnginePartRecord>`
     - One painted part's record.
   * - :cpp:class:`EnginePartTable <gui_forms::EnginePartTable>`
     - The table.
   * - :cpp:class:`EnginePrimitive <gui_forms::EnginePrimitive>`
     - One kind of effect.
   * - :cpp:class:`EngineSineCosine <gui_forms::EngineSineCosine>`
     - The sine and cosine of ``x`` radians, computed together (E3's springs).
   * - :cpp:class:`EngineVersion <gui_forms::EngineVersion>`
     - The engine version a primitive was introduced in, and the version a theme declares it needs.
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionCompilation <gui_forms::ExpressionCompilation>`
     - A compilation's outcome: the program when ``defect`` is none, otherwise the defect and the position of the instruction it concerns (0 for a defect of the whole program).
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionDependencies <gui_forms::ExpressionDependencies>`
     - What a program reads, found when it is compiled: bit i of ``inputs`` for the input at position i, bit i of ``parameters`` for the parameter at position i, and the standard states in ``states``.
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionEnvironment <gui_forms::ExpressionEnvironment>`
     - What one evaluation reads.
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionInputIndex <gui_forms::ExpressionInputIndex>`
     - The position of an input in the inputs an expression is evaluated with.
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionInstruction <gui_forms::ExpressionInstruction>`
     - One instruction.
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionLimits <gui_forms::ExpressionLimits>`
     - How many inputs and parameters the program may address: an index at or beyond either count is refused when the program is compiled.
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionParameterIndex <gui_forms::ExpressionParameterIndex>`
     - The position of a number parameter among a primitive's declared parameters (PrimitiveDescriptor::parameters).
   * - :cpp:class:`ExpressionResult <gui_forms::ExpressionResult>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`FamiliarThemeBuild <gui_forms::FamiliarThemeBuild>`
     - A built theme and what building it measured.
   * - :cpp:class:`FinishFill <gui_forms::FinishFill>`
     - One finish's parameters.
   * - :cpp:class:`FinishTileKey <gui_forms::FinishTileKey>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`FinishTiles <gui_forms::FinishTiles>`
     - A surface finish over an area, in world coordinates (ThemeEngine::paint_finish_tiles; cp31, the node canvas's finish texture): - ``key`` names the part's record (borrowed for the call); - ``finish`` is borrowed for the call; - ``anchor`` is world (0, 0) in the painter's coordinates and ``zoom`` the view units per world unit, so the finish is a property of the world: it pans with it and grows with the zoom; - ``area`` is where it is drawn, clipped to it (the painter's coordinates).
   * - :cpp:class:`FontDeclaration <gui_forms::FontDeclaration>`
     - A part's font as the cascade resolved it: a text role, or, with ``inherit`` (D7), the parent control's, which the caller resolves at paint.
   * - :cpp:class:`FontSpec <gui_forms::FontSpec>`
     - FontSpec carries logical role, size, weight, italic, and bounded letter-spacing inputs shared by measurement and painting.
   * - :cpp:class:`GlossColours <gui_forms::GlossColours>`
     - The colours of a gloss material, top to bottom.
   * - :cpp:class:`GradientStop <gui_forms::GradientStop>`
     - GradientStop pairs a normalized monotonic offset with a copied color for retained display-list safety.
   * - :cpp:class:`GridLevel <gui_forms::GridLevel>`
     - The level chosen for one zoom: ``level`` is k, ``minor_spacing`` the level's spacing in view units, and ``fade`` the spacing's position across the band, 0 at its minimum and 1 at its maximum (linear in the spacing).
   * - :cpp:class:`GridLevelBand <gui_forms::GridLevelBand>`
     - Seamless deep zoom for the grid (NODE_ENGINE_AND_VALUE_BAR_STUDY 2.10): the grid has levels of world spacing ``world_spacing * 4^k``; the level shown is the one whose spacing in view units (times ``zoom``) lies in [minimum_spacing, maximum_spacing), which must span exactly the factor 4.
   * - :cpp:class:`GridPattern <gui_forms::GridPattern>`
     - A world-aligned grid (piece C17; the node canvas's blueprint background): lines ``spacing`` units apart through ``origin``, the point where world (0, 0) lies in the painter's current coordinates, so the lines move with a pan; every ``major_every``-th line counted from the origin (1: every line) is a major line in ``major``, the others are minor lines in ``minor``.
   * - :cpp:class:`GuiFormsThemeBuild <gui_forms::GuiFormsThemeBuild>`
     - The built theme and what building it measured.
   * - :cpp:class:`ImageId <gui_forms::ImageId>`
     - ImageId is the nonzero renderer-neutral identity returned by a Window image registry.
   * - :cpp:class:`ImageVariantRequest <gui_forms::ImageVariantRequest>`
     - One sized variant of an image resource: the image resampled to ``extent`` device pixels, for display ``scale``.
   * - :cpp:class:`InlineStyleResult <gui_forms::InlineStyleResult>`
     - What setting a control's inline style gave (Control::set_inline_style): applied, with any warnings (none for empty text, which clears the style), or refused with its diagnostics, the control keeping its previous inline style.
   * - :cpp:class:`Insets <gui_forms::Insets>`
     - Insets stores independent logical left, top, right, and bottom extents for margin, padding, damage, and visual outsets.
   * - :cpp:class:`ItemContentPaint <gui_forms::ItemContentPaint>`
     - One item's content to paint (owner decision W-Q6), raised while the control paints, after the item's plate and selection and before its focus cue.
   * - :cpp:class:`JoinedSegmentsStyle <gui_forms::JoinedSegmentsStyle>`
     - How a joined row or column is outlined: one outer edge round the whole group with ``radius`` corners, and one shared seam between neighbours, both ``edge_width`` wide.
   * - :cpp:class:`LampColour <gui_forms::engine_light::LampColour>`
     - A lamp's colour, linear RGB at unit power.
   * - :cpp:class:`LampDrive <gui_forms::engine_light::LampDrive>`
     - A lamp as composited: its colour and its power (0 off, 1 the baked unit; the strike envelope passes through, lamp_power).
   * - :cpp:class:`LentImageVariants <gui_forms::LentImageVariants>`
     - The variants lent for one bake, by fill layer; null where none is lent.
   * - :cpp:class:`LightComposite <gui_forms::engine_light::LightComposite>`
     - The composite's settings, defaulting to the owner's panel_lamps choices: the room at gain 1 and the tone's exposure 1.3.
   * - :cpp:class:`LightPlaneCache <gui_forms::engine_light::LightPlaneCache>`
     - The bakes in one directory, each read the first time it is asked for (``<directory>/<name>.f32``) and kept: a missing or defective file is remembered and not read again.
   * - :cpp:class:`LightPlaneRaster <gui_forms::engine_light::LightPlaneRaster>`
     - -- a part's kept composite ----------------------------------------------------------------- A part composited from a bake (PartPaint::light_planes, such as IndicatorLamp's lens) keeps its last composite in one of these, owned by the part's control, so that a repaint with nothing changed (a lamp repainted for a neighbour's damage) draws the kept raster again instead of compositing.
   * - :cpp:class:`LightPlaneRect <gui_forms::engine_light::LightPlaneRect>`
     - A rectangle of device pixels the planes are stretched over.
   * - :cpp:class:`LightPlanes <gui_forms::engine_light::LightPlanes>`
     - One bake's planes, ``width`` × ``height`` pixels: - ``room``: the room's light, three floats a pixel (red, green, blue), premultiplied by coverage; - ``lamps``: ``lamp_count`` planes of the same shape, one after another, each lamp alone at unit white power; - ``coverage``: one float a pixel in [0, 1], the share of the pixel the key covers.
   * - :cpp:class:`LitKeyBake <gui_forms::engine_light::LitKeyBake>`
     - -- the lit keys' bake ------------------------------------------------------------------ One lit key's bake parameters, and the values the study baked its dialog keys with (lamp_keys.cpp, the dialog family): - height above the panel as a share of the key's short side: 0.57 there, an early test value.
   * - :cpp:class:`MarkerStyle <gui_forms::MarkerStyle>`
     - A marker drawn centred on each point: ``size`` its full width, ``fill`` its inside (ignored by the stroked shapes), ``edge`` its outline (or its lines, for the stroked shapes) ``edge_width`` wide.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialAssemblyContext <gui_forms::MaterialAssemblyContext>`
     - Replaces ``material`` with ``floor`` and the declarations of ``fields`` (the winning declaration of each field, by the field's position, null where none) in place: a part's assembled material (EC1); an ``initial`` declaration keeps the floor's value.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialBlocks <gui_forms::MaterialBlocks>`
     - A material repeated over an area (ThemeEngine::paint_material_blocks): - ``key`` names the part's record (borrowed for the call); - ``material`` is borrowed for the call; - ``period`` is the material's repeat in logical units (a procedural fill's tile), within [minimum_procedural_tile, maximum_procedural_tile]; - the blocks lie on the lattice of whole periods through ``anchor``, so that they stay where the caller anchors them as it pans, and are drawn where they meet ``area``, clipped to it (the painter's coordinates).
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialBorder <gui_forms::MaterialBorder>`
     - MaterialBorder pairs an authored color and logical stroke width.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialBorderEdges <gui_forms::MaterialBorderEdges>`
     - Single edges (CSS border-color with differing sides).
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialColourFilter <gui_forms::MaterialColourFilter>`
     - A material's colour filter: none, or one matrix held out of line.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialColourMatrix <gui_forms::MaterialColourMatrix>`
     - A colour filter (E9.6; CSS ``filter`` and ``backdrop-filter`` colour functions): a 4 × 5 matrix on straight (not premultiplied) colour with each channel in [0, 1].
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialCornerRadii <gui_forms::MaterialCornerRadii>`
     - A material's four corner radii (E9.6, CSS ``border-*-radius``), in logical units: top-left, top-right, bottom-right and bottom-left.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialDeclaration <gui_forms::MaterialDeclaration>`
     - One material field a rule sets and its value.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialDropShadow <gui_forms::MaterialDropShadow>`
     - A drop shadow of the part's own drawn alpha (E9.6, CSS ``filter: drop-shadow()``): the baked raster's alpha, blurred by ``blur_radius`` (σ = blur / 2, as a shadow's), moved by ``offset``, in ``color``, drawn beneath the raster.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialFillLayer <gui_forms::MaterialFillLayer>`
     - MaterialFillLayer is one renderer-neutral solid, gradient, or image recipe with explicit coordinate space, spread/wrap, source geometry, scale, and opacity.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialGlow <gui_forms::MaterialGlow>`
     - A soft coloured emission of the material's box (E9-C): the box grown by ``spread`` on every side and blurred by ``blur_radius`` (σ = blur_radius / 2, as a shadow's), centred on the box.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialImagePosition <gui_forms::MaterialImagePosition>`
     - An image layer's position (CSS ``background-position``): each axis's fraction is the layer's image_anchor, of the room the box leaves around one tile, and its units its image_offset.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialImageSize <gui_forms::MaterialImageSize>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialKeyline <gui_forms::MaterialKeyline>`
     - One ordered physical keyline.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialReflection <gui_forms::MaterialReflection>`
     - A reflection (stage T5, C7; owner T5-Q14, T5-Q15): ``source`` within the part's box, mirrored about the box's ``edge``, ``gap`` logical units beyond it, for ``reach`` units, faded by a linear opacity from ``start_opacity`` next to the edge to ``end_opacity`` at the far end.
   * - :cpp:class:`MaterialShadow <gui_forms::MaterialShadow>`
     - MaterialShadow specifies offset, blur, spread, and color for one bounded surface shadow.
   * - :cpp:class:`MediaCondition <gui_forms::MediaCondition>`
     - A rule's media condition (EC3): every feature it names must hold for the window, and an unnamed feature always holds, so the default condition holds everywhere.
   * - :cpp:class:`MediaFacts <gui_forms::MediaFacts>`
     - What a window is, as a rule's media condition reads it (T3 EC3): its presentation settings and its scale.
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionClock <gui_forms::MotionClock>`
     - One window's motion clock (T2 plan, MotionClock; GIVEN D1-1).
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionCurve <gui_forms::MotionCurve>`
     - How a value moves.
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionRecordHandle <gui_forms::MotionRecordHandle>`
     - Which record of a clock a value holds while it moves: a slot and the slot's generation when the record was made.
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionStep <gui_forms::MotionStep>`
     - One step of a value, as its listener sees it.
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionStop <gui_forms::MotionStop>`
     - One intermediate stop of a timed move (E9.6, multi-stop @keyframes): the value passes ``value`` at ``offset`` of the move's duration, 0 < offset < 1.
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionTimeSource <gui_forms::MotionTimeSource>`
     - Where a clock reads the time a motion starts.
   * - :cpp:class:`MotionValue <gui_forms::MotionValue>`
     - One value.
   * - :cpp:class:`PaintCornerRadii <gui_forms::PaintCornerRadii>`
     - A rounded rectangle's four corner radii, in the rectangle's units: top-left, top-right, bottom-right and bottom-left.
   * - :cpp:class:`Painter <gui_forms::Painter>`
     - Painter is the renderer-neutral command vocabulary for transforms, clips, solids, rounded geometry, gradients, shadows, text, registered images, live surfaces, sampled regions, and bounded patterns; rich operations provide coherent minimal-host fallbacks.
   * - :cpp:class:`PaintGroup <gui_forms::PaintGroup>`
     - A paint group (T2 plan, E7; owner, 2026-10-21: "there are other things that group layer may be needed for"): commands drawn as one image, then composited once.
   * - :cpp:class:`PaintSegment <gui_forms::PaintSegment>`
     - One independent segment of stroke_segments (no joins between segments).
   * - :cpp:class:`ParameterValidation <gui_forms::ParameterValidation>`
     - The first defect found and the index of the parameter it concerns.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartArc <gui_forms::PartArc>`
     - An arc's or a needle's angles (design language 8.5; row 2.5, owner option (a)), in radians clockwise from twelve o'clock, on the circle inscribed in the part's bounds (centred in them, its diameter their shorter side).
   * - :cpp:class:`PartCornerRadii <gui_forms::PartCornerRadii>`
     - The four corners' radii of a shape drawn in a box, in the box's units: top-left, top-right, bottom-right and bottom-left.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartHitRegion <gui_forms::PartHitRegion>`
     - A part's hit region (T1's hand-off): its shape in its bounds, never less than the minimum target.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartKey <gui_forms::PartKey>`
     - A painted part's identity from frame to frame (T2 GIVEN H3-1), which lets the engine keep a record for it: its states and when it entered them, its transitions, its preparation and its cached raster.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartLightPlanes <gui_forms::PartLightPlanes>`
     - A part composited from baked light planes (skeudoindustrial surfaces; engine/light_planes): the bake, the one drive every lamp of it takes, and the composite its control keeps between paints.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartMotion <gui_forms::PartMotion>`
     - A part's running transitions (E7; T2 plan, "How motion is driven").
   * - :cpp:class:`PartPaint <gui_forms::PartPaint>`
     - One part to paint, in the control's local coordinates.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartProfile <gui_forms::PartProfile>`
     - A part's profile beside its rectangle (look integration; the T4 rendering reference's tab stack and the owner's Plan Paint tab look), as a theme sets it on the part (ControlPartMetrics) and the control passes it on: - ``taper``: the trailing (right) edge leans in by this many units at the part's top, a file-folder tab's edge, drawn as a line in ``taper_colour`` when it has one; - ``flare``: the bottom corners flare outward by this many units in a concave lip of ``flare_colour``, where a selected tab meets the plane; - ``edge_shadow``: a soft shadow beyond the trailing edge, following its taper, falling off from ``edge_shadow_colour`` to clear over this many units.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartRaster <gui_forms::PartRaster>`
     - One part's pixels as the engine keeps them (T2 plan, PartRaster): premultiplied BGRA8 in device pixels, in PartRasterView's layout, made for one display scale.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartRasterRequest <gui_forms::PartRasterRequest>`
     - What a part needs drawn: the primitive a rule selected, its parameters, the part's target, and the destination extent in device pixels (normally the logical size grown by the primitive's outsets, times the scale, rounded up).
   * - :cpp:class:`PartRasterResult <gui_forms::PartRasterResult>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`PartRasterView <gui_forms::PartRasterView>`
     - A borrowed destination for one part's pixels.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartRecordAcquisition <gui_forms::PartRecordAcquisition>`
     - What acquire() did.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartRecordHandle <gui_forms::PartRecordHandle>`
     - Which record a handle names: a slot of the table and the slot's generation when the record was made.
   * - :cpp:class:`PartShape <gui_forms::PartShape>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`PartTransitionChannel <gui_forms::PartTransitionChannel>`
     - One property's transition on a part (E7): an engine property or the parameter at ``parameter``, the machine and the state of it the part was last painted in (state none when the part's states matched none), and the property's motion value, made the first time the part is painted with the transition.
   * - :cpp:class:`Point <gui_forms::Point>`
     - Point is a renderer-neutral logical-coordinate pair.
   * - :cpp:class:`PointerOverlay <gui_forms::PointerOverlay>`
     - How a primitive behind the overlay seam is composited (E9-A, owner 2026-10-22: the seam serves the sheen only).
   * - :cpp:class:`PresentationState <gui_forms::PresentationState>`
     - One state of a part's presentational state machine (E7; design language §9.6).
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveCost <gui_forms::PrimitiveCost>`
     - A declared upper bound on a primitive's work.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveDescriptor <gui_forms::PrimitiveDescriptor>`
     - What a primitive is.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveParameterBlock <gui_forms::PrimitiveParameterBlock>`
     - The values of one part's parameters: ``count`` live values, one per declared parameter, in declaration order.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveParameterDeclaration <gui_forms::PrimitiveParameterDeclaration>`
     - One declared parameter.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveParameterValue <gui_forms::PrimitiveParameterValue>`
     - One parameter's value.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitivePreparation <gui_forms::PrimitivePreparation>`
     - What a primitive derives once from a part's geometry and fixed parameters, and reuses for every rasterization in which only animated parameters change.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveRegistry <gui_forms::PrimitiveRegistry>`
     - A fixed table of primitives, in registration order.
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveSlicePlan <gui_forms::PrimitiveSlicePlan>`
     - How a part's raster may be assembled (EnginePrimitive::slice_plan).
   * - :cpp:class:`PrimitiveTarget <gui_forms::PrimitiveTarget>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ProceduralFill <gui_forms::ProceduralFill>`

   * - :cpp:class:`PropertyTransition <gui_forms::PropertyTransition>`
     - A transition on one resolved property: ``opacity``, or a number parameter of the rule's primitive that the primitive declares animated (such as the bulge's amplitude), so that its frames never remake the part's preparation.
   * - :cpp:class:`RasterCacheStatistics <gui_forms::RasterCacheStatistics>`
     - The raster cache's counters, for instrumentation.
   * - :cpp:class:`RecipeDeclaration <gui_forms::RecipeDeclaration>`
     - One recipe field's declared value.
   * - :cpp:class:`Rect <gui_forms::Rect>`
     - Rect is a half-open logical rectangle with finite/empty checks, containment, exact intersection, and bounding union operations.
   * - :cpp:class:`ResolvedControlRecipe <gui_forms::ResolvedControlRecipe>`
     - A control's recipe for one role and state (T3 P6f): the theme's built-in recipe (the floor, Theme::resolve(role, context), borrowed from the theme) or, when the theme's rules set a recipe field for the control (RecipeField), the floor's copy with those fields in place, which this value owns.
   * - :cpp:class:`ResolvedFont <gui_forms::ResolvedFont>`
     - A control's primary text font as resolved (Theme::resolve_font, Control::resolved_text_font), before the window's text scale: the font the control holds with the theme's font fields over it.
   * - :cpp:class:`RetainedDrawCache <gui_forms::RetainedDrawCache>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`RetainedDrawMemo <gui_forms::RetainedDrawMemo>`
     - Renderer-derived data for one retained draw command, such as shaped text.
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleAncestor <gui_forms::RuleAncestor>`
     - One ancestor compound of a selector (selector step 4): a control type, a stable ID and classes, each optional, matched against an ancestor control.
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleDeclaration <gui_forms::RuleDeclaration>`
     - One declaration: a property and its value.
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleDiagnostic <gui_forms::RuleDiagnostic>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleExclusion <gui_forms::RuleExclusion>`
     - One argument of a selector's ``:not()`` (selector step 2): a compound of a control type, a stable ID, classes, states and a family, each optional; it excludes the parts it matches.
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleProvenance <gui_forms::RuleProvenance>`
     - Where a rule was written (T4 cascade spec, item 5), for an editor's queries; paint never reads it.
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleSelector <gui_forms::RuleSelector>`
     - ``family``, when set, is a control visual family's registered name (the theme's ControlVisualRole, such as ``accent_button``): the selector matches only parts resolved for that family (PartFacts::family).
   * - :cpp:class:`RuleSpecificity <gui_forms::RuleSpecificity>`
     - A selector's specificity, CSS's three columns compared in order: the stable ID and the item's ID; the classes, the item's classes, the states (standard and custom) and the family (pseudo-classes); the control type or surface and the part (its name or role, one each, as pseudo-elements).
   * - :cpp:class:`ScopedCondition <gui_forms::ScopedCondition>`
     - A copy's condition: the part's winning definition of ``name`` is ``definition``, or, for custom_property_undefined, no definition matches.
   * - :cpp:class:`ScopedDefinition <gui_forms::ScopedDefinition>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`Size <gui_forms::Size>`
     - Size is a renderer-neutral logical width and height.
   * - :cpp:class:`SliceAxis <gui_forms::SliceAxis>`
     - One axis of a slice plan.
   * - :cpp:class:`StrokeStyle <gui_forms::StrokeStyle>`
     - A stroke's colour, width, joins, caps and dashes, in the painter's current coordinates.
   * - :cpp:class:`SurfaceMaterial <gui_forms::SurfaceMaterial>`
     - SurfaceMaterial is a bounded ordered fill stack plus optional border, bounded shadow stack, and shared corner radius.
   * - :cpp:class:`Theme <gui_forms::Theme>`
     - Theme is an immutable validated role/state recipe table plus structural spacing, geometry, typography, and motion tokens; it resolves logical visual context without exposing renderer or platform objects.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeBackdropBase <gui_forms::ThemeBackdropBase>`
     - One backplane base: the material a window's backplane is drawn from while it is granted ``backdrop`` (ThemeWindowBackdrop).
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeCompileDiagnostic <gui_forms::ThemeCompileDiagnostic>`
     - One finding of an install or a compilation.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeCompileOptions <gui_forms::ThemeCompileOptions>`
     - What a compilation reads besides the package.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeCompileResult <gui_forms::ThemeCompileResult>`
     - What compiling a theme package gave: compiled, a theme with any warnings, or refused with its diagnostics.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeDefinition <gui_forms::ThemeDefinition>`
     - ThemeDefinition is one named, complete compatibility palette, role/state material matrix, and structural-token set.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeEngine <gui_forms::ThemeEngine>`
     - The theme engine's entry point (T2 plan, ThemeEngine).
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeEngine::PartPlan <gui_forms::ThemeEngine::PartPlan>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeGeometryTokens <gui_forms::ThemeGeometryTokens>`
     - ThemeGeometryTokens centralizes control heights, touch targets, splitter geometry, navigation extents, and compact breakpoint.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeItem <gui_forms::ThemeItem>`
     - An application colour role's colours where some media facts hold (Theme::application_role, Control::theme_role_colours): its foreground, its background for a pair (the foreground's plate for a single role), the built-in role it falls back to and whether it is a pair.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeLibrary <gui_forms::ThemeLibrary>`
     - The installed theme packages an ``@import`` may name (D2): each verified whole when installed (its manifest, every file's hash, and every ``@import`` of its sheet listed in its manifest), and found by its name.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeMotionTokens <gui_forms::ThemeMotionTokens>`
     - ThemeMotionTokens defines quick, standard, emphasized, and busy-cycle durations without prescribing an animation engine.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemePackage <gui_forms::ThemePackage>`
     - A theme package: its manifest's bytes and its files.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemePackageExport <gui_forms::ThemePackageExport>`
     - One of the program's packages as a user theme's files, for export (USER_THEMES.md section 7): its sheet verbatim after an ``@theme`` header of its manifest's version, author, provenance and engine bound, and its ``.png`` resources as the folder's images.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemePackageFile <gui_forms::ThemePackageFile>`
     - One file of a theme package: its path inside the package, as the manifest lists it, and its bytes.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeRoleColours <gui_forms::ThemeRoleColours>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeRule <gui_forms::ThemeRule>`
     -  
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeRuleSheet <gui_forms::ThemeRuleSheet>`
     - A theme's rules, as authored, with the engine version the theme declares it needs and the values an application publishes that its expressions read (E6; Window::publish_value).
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeSpacingTokens <gui_forms::ThemeSpacingTokens>`
     - ThemeSpacingTokens names the shared micro-through-section logical spacing scale.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeStructureTokens <gui_forms::ThemeStructureTokens>`
     - ThemeStructureTokens groups spacing, geometry, typography, and motion vocabularies into one coherent structural contract.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeTypographyTokens <gui_forms::ThemeTypographyTokens>`
     - ThemeTypographyTokens defines role-aware control, field, caption, heading, title, and monospace font recipes.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeValidation <gui_forms::ThemeValidation>`
     - What validating theme packages found (validate_theme_packages): whether they compile, and every finding, warnings included.
   * - :cpp:class:`ThemeWindowBackdrop <gui_forms::ThemeWindowBackdrop>`
     - The theme's window backdrop (stage T5, owner decision T5-Q8, option 1): what a window showing the theme asks the compositor for, and the base its backplane is drawn from over each backdrop the window may be granted (notes:planning/stages/T5-compositor/PLAN.md §4.1).
   * - :cpp:class:`UnkeptPartAssembly <gui_forms::UnkeptPartAssembly>`
     - One of a window's assemblies for parts that keep none of their own (a part without a record, and every part's derived dark variant): the assembly, the role whose dark variant darkened it (none for a light assembly) and whether it holds one yet.
   * - :cpp:class:`UserThemeFiles <gui_forms::UserThemeFiles>`
     - A user theme's files (T3 user themes, notes:planning/stages/ T3-theme-language/USER_THEMES.md): its name, the stem of its main file ``themes/<name>.css``; that file's bytes; and the files of its folder ``themes/<name>/``, each by its path inside the folder, as the host read them.
   * - :cpp:class:`UserThemeHeader <gui_forms::UserThemeHeader>`
     - The header of a user theme's main file, which makes the file a theme.
   * - :cpp:class:`VisualInteraction <gui_forms::VisualInteraction>`
     - A control's interaction state as it asks for its visual context (Control::visual_context, audit A13): named fields, all false by default.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: AmbientEffect final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/theme_rules/theme_rules.hpp>``

   An ambient effect (E7): its part repaints as time passes, at most ``frames_per_second``, and reads time_in_state within one ``period`` (it wraps), so that the effect cycles. Its region is the part's extent. It runs only while a primitive draws the part and the part has a record, and stops in an occluded or minimized window, under reduced motion and while the application's motion switch is off (PresentationSettings::ambient_motion); the part then rests at ``pose``, the time within the period it shows when still, such as an activity object's static pose.

   .. cpp:member:: FrameInterval period

   .. cpp:member:: double frames_per_second

   .. cpp:member:: FrameInterval pose

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: AncestorBloom final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/theme_rules/theme_rules.hpp>``

   The keys an ancestor chain holds, hashed into 256 bits: a rule whose ancestor keys are not all present cannot match, and is rejected without walking the chain (CSS engines' ancestor Bloom filter).


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:function:: void add(std::string_view prefix, std::string_view key) noexcept

      Adds ``key``, written as a selector writes it: ``type``, ``#id`` or ``.class``.

   .. cpp:function:: bool holds(const AncestorBloom& needed) const noexcept

   .. cpp:member:: std::array<std::uint64_t, 4> bits

.. cpp:struct:: AncestorFacts final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/theme_rules/theme_rules.hpp>``

   One ancestor control as a selector's ancestor compounds read it: borrowed from the control for the resolution.

   .. cpp:member:: std::string_view control_type

   .. cpp:member:: std::string_view stable_id

   .. cpp:member:: std::span<const std::string> classes

.. cpp:struct:: ApplicationColourRole final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/theme_rules/theme_rules.hpp>``

   An application role as the theme compiled it: its name, its fallback built-in role, whether it is a pair (its fallback's shape), and its declarations in sheet order from its last unconditional one (the base colours, where there is one), each later one holding where its condition does. A role without an unconditional declaration carries its fallback's declarations before its own.

   .. cpp:member:: std::string name

   .. cpp:member:: ColourRole fallback

      Default: ``{ColourRole::ink_on_paper}``.

   .. cpp:member:: bool paired

   .. cpp:member:: std::vector<ApplicationColourRoleVariant> variants

.. cpp:struct:: ApplicationColourRoleVariant final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/theme_rules/theme_rules.hpp>``

   One declaration of an application role's colours: its foreground and, for a pair, its background, where ``media`` holds.

   .. cpp:member:: MediaCondition media

   .. cpp:member:: Color foreground

   .. cpp:member:: Color background

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: AssembledPartMaterial final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/part_table/engine_part_table.hpp>``

   A part's assembled material (T3 EC1): its recipe's material, the floor, with the winning material fields of the theme's rules in place, and what it was assembled from. It is assembled again only when that changes: - ``rules_serial`` and ``fields``: the compiled rules' serial (CompiledThemeRules::serial) and the winning declaration of each field, by address within those rules (null for none); - ``floor``: a copy of the recipe material, compared by value, since the theme's recipe may be replaced and another may occupy its address; - ``box``: the part's box size, for fields that read it (material_fields_read_box), else empty.

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t rules_serial

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t inline_serial

      The serial of the part's inline style, or 0 (T3 P9).

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t user_serial

      The serial of the part's user theme, or 0 (T3 user themes).

   .. cpp:member:: std::array<const MaterialDeclaration*, material_field_count> fields

   .. cpp:member:: Size box

   .. cpp:member:: double em

      The part's em and text colour, where a field reads them (T3 P8c); else 0 and transparent black.

   .. cpp:member:: Color current_colour

   .. cpp:member:: SurfaceMaterial floor

   .. cpp:member:: SurfaceMaterial material

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: AuthoredGradient final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/authored_gradient/authored_gradient.hpp>``

   A gradient as written: - ``kind``: linear_gradient, radial_gradient or conic_gradient; - ``repeating``: CSS's repeating- form; - ``angle_degrees``: a linear gradient's CSS angle (0 up, 90 right), unless ``corner`` names one, or a conic gradient's ``from``; - ``centre_x``, ``centre_y``: a radial or conic gradient's centre, across and down the box; - ``circle``, ``extent``, ``radius_x``, ``radius_y``: a radial gradient's shape and size; with ``lengths``, an ellipse's radii, each along its own axis, or a circle's radius, radius_x's units; - ``stops``: at least one, at most maximum_authored_gradient_stops.

   .. cpp:member:: MaterialFillKind kind

      Default: ``{MaterialFillKind::linear_gradient}``.

   .. cpp:member:: bool repeating

   .. cpp:member:: double angle_degrees

      Default: ``{180.0}``.

   .. cpp:member:: GradientCorner corner

      Default: ``{GradientCorner::none}``.

   .. cpp:member:: BoxLength centre_x

      Default: ``{0.5, 0.0}``.

   .. cpp:member:: BoxLength centre_y

      Default: ``{0.5, 0.0}``.

   .. cpp:member:: bool circle

   .. cpp:member:: RadialExtent extent

      Default: ``{RadialExtent::farthest_corner}``.

   .. cpp:member:: BoxLength radius_x

   .. cpp:member:: BoxLength radius_y

   .. cpp:member:: std::vector<AuthoredGradientStop> stops

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: AuthoredGradientStop final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/authored_gradient/authored_gradient.hpp>``

   One colour stop. ``position``, when ``positioned``, is along the gradient: - linear: a fraction of the gradient line plus logical units along it; - radial: a fraction of the gradient ray (the horizontal radius) plus logical units along it; - conic: a fraction of a turn (``units`` is 0). A stop without a position is placed as CSS places it.

   .. cpp:member:: Color colour

   .. cpp:member:: bool positioned

   .. cpp:member:: BoxLength position

   ``#include <gui_forms/types/paint_types/paint_types.hpp>``

   A bundled font family a theme names in place of a role's faces (theme-language ``font-family: Selawik``). Zero is none: the role's own faces. Only families bundled with the library have numbers (the table in src/core/resources/fonts/bundled_font_families.hpp); a host font never has one, so a family can never reach a font the application did not ship.

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint8_t value

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: CachedPartRaster final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/part_table/engine_part_table.hpp>``

   A part's cached raster and what drew it: the primitive, every parameter (animated ones included, since they change the pixels), the target with its states restricted to those the primitive reads, and the extent. The record owns it; ``raster`` is the cache's reference, and recorded commands that composite the raster hold others. A material the target lent is kept as a copy, compared by value: the theme's recipe that lent it may be replaced, and another recipe may later occupy its address. The kept target's borrowed pointers are cleared. When the material has an image fill, ``image_revision`` is the image registry's revision it was drawn at, so any change to the registry redraws it.

   .. cpp:member:: std::shared_ptr<PartRaster> raster

   .. cpp:member:: const EnginePrimitive* primitive

   .. cpp:member:: PrimitiveParameterBlock parameters

   .. cpp:member:: PrimitiveTarget target

   .. cpp:member:: DeviceExtent extent

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<SurfaceMaterial> material

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t image_revision

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<std::uint64_t> awaited_patterns

      Set when ``raster`` drew a procedural fill's ground because its pattern was not ready (E10): the table's pattern generation then. Such a raster is current only until the table makes another pattern.

   .. cpp:member:: bool shared

      Whether ``raster`` is one of the table's shared bakes (a keyframe): it then counts against shared_bake_budget_bytes, not the part cache's, and is never drawn over.

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t raster_generation

      The record's and the table's raster generations when ``raster`` was drawn; invalidation advances the current ones.

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t raster_table_generation

   .. cpp:member:: bool listed

      N37: the table's recency list of part rasters it budgets (held, not shared), least recently painted first. Observing links, kept by the table; ``listed`` says whether this raster is on the list.

   .. cpp:member:: CachedPartRaster* older_raster

   .. cpp:member:: CachedPartRaster* newer_raster

Both views are borrowed for the call.

The marquee is its area (``marquee``) and its dashed outline (``marquee_outline``; no halo). A plain value: copy it, keep it, compare it.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:class:: CompiledThemeRules final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/theme_rules/theme_rules.hpp>``

   A sheet compiled against a registry: the rules that survived, each declaration checked, in the order the cascade visits them. Immutable once made; a ThemeEngine owns one. Compiling never fails as a whole: an invalid declaration or rule is dropped with a diagnostic and the rest stands, so a broken rule cannot take the theme's other rules down with it.


   .. rubric:: Construction

   .. cpp:function:: CompiledThemeRules()

   .. cpp:function:: CompiledThemeRules(const CompiledThemeRules& other)

   .. cpp:function:: CompiledThemeRules(CompiledThemeRules&& other) noexcept = default


   .. rubric:: Properties

   .. cpp:function:: std::uint64_t theme_serial() const noexcept

      The serial of the theme an inline style was compiled against, 0 for none (P12b).

   .. cpp:function:: std::size_t read_name_count() const noexcept

      How many custom property names the rules read (P12b).

   .. cpp:function:: bool inline_style() const noexcept

      Whether the rules are a control's inline style (compile_inline).

   .. cpp:function:: bool user_theme() const noexcept

      Whether the rules are a user theme (compile_user).

   .. cpp:function:: std::size_t built_in_rule_count() const noexcept

      How many of the rules are the engine's built-in rules.

   .. cpp:function:: bool uses_ancestors() const noexcept

      Whether a rule has ancestor compounds (selector step 4): its facts need the control's ancestors, and a change to an ancestor's type, ID or classes, or a move, re-resolves the control's subtree.

   .. cpp:function:: bool empty() const noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: std::size_t rule_count() const noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: std::size_t scoped_name_count() const noexcept

      How many scoped custom property names the rules' conditions read (P12).

   .. cpp:function:: const std::vector<RuleDiagnostic>& diagnostics() const& noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: const std::vector<std::string>& published_values() const& noexcept

      The published values the sheet's expressions read, in position order (an invalid name is kept empty, so later positions stay).

   .. cpp:function:: std::size_t input_count() const noexcept

      How many inputs the sheet's expressions are evaluated with: the engine inputs and the sheet's published values.

   .. cpp:function:: std::uint64_t serial() const noexcept

      This compilation's serial number, unique among the compilations of the process: a cache keyed by the addresses of the rules' declarations (an assembled material, EC1) compares it first. A copy takes a new one, since its declarations lie elsewhere; a move keeps it with the declarations.


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:function:: static CompiledThemeRules compile(const ThemeRuleSheet& sheet, const PrimitiveRegistry& registry)

      Compiles ``sheet`` against ``registry``, which must outlive the result.

   .. cpp:function:: static CompiledThemeRules compile_with_built_in(const ThemeRuleSheet& sheet, const PrimitiveRegistry& registry)

      Compiles the engine's built-in rules (built_in_theme_rules, at CascadeLevel::built_in, below every authored rule) and then ``sheet``. The theme engine's rules are made this way. Diagnostics name the sheet's rules by their positions in ``sheet``.

   .. cpp:function:: static CompiledThemeRules compile_inline(const ThemeRuleSheet& sheet, const PrimitiveRegistry& registry, const CompiledThemeRules* theme = nullptr)

      Compiles one control's inline style (T3 P9, R2-10): rules at :cpp:func:`inline_style <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::inline_style>` and ``important_inline_style`` only, each without a stable ID, since the control owns them and consults them for itself alone (PartFacts::inline_rules). The sheet publishes no values. Compiled against ``theme`` (P12b), the style also holds copies of the theme's rules that read its custom properties (each with a source_position, at the theme's authored levels): the theme's rules are then passed over wherever the style's definition of a name they read wins, while the style is used with ``theme`` itself (the same compilation, by serial). ``theme`` is read during the call only.

   .. cpp:function:: static CompiledThemeRules compile_user(const ThemeRuleSheet& sheet, const PrimitiveRegistry& registry)

      Compiles a user theme (USER_THEMES.md section 4): rules at :cpp:func:`user_theme <gui_forms::CompiledThemeRules::user_theme>` and ``important_user_theme`` only, a set of their own that every part of a program's windows consults beside its theme's (PartFacts::user_rules). Its opacity is at least user_theme_minimum_opacity. It publishes no values, so its expressions read the engine inputs alone, and its custom properties are its own.

   .. cpp:function:: std::optional<ExpressionInputIndex> published_value_input(std::string_view name) const noexcept

      The position of the published value ``name``, or none when the sheet does not read it.

   .. cpp:function:: RecipeResolution resolve_recipe_fields(const PartFacts& facts) const noexcept

      The recipe fields' cascade for a control: ``facts`` name no part and the region role. Linear in the number of rules, and at once when no rule sets a recipe field.

   .. cpp:function:: void resolve_provenance(const PartFacts& facts, PartProvenance& result) const noexcept

      Fills ``result`` with the provenance of the part ``facts`` name, for an editor's query: linear in the number of rules, allocating nothing. Paint never calls it. The borrows in ``result`` stay valid while these rules and the facts' inline style and user theme live.

   .. cpp:function:: const BackdropDeclaration* resolve_backdrop(const PartFacts& facts) const noexcept

      The winning backdrop of the surface ``facts`` name (PartFacts::surface, its root control's classes and ID): the last matching rule's, which belongs to the compiled rules or to the user theme's, or null where no matching rule declares one. ``backdrop: none`` wins as a chain of length 0. Linear in the number of rules.

   .. cpp:function:: BandDeclaration resolve_band(const PartFacts& facts) const noexcept

      The band of the window ``facts`` name (PartFacts::surface window, its root control's classes and ID): each field from the last matching rule that sets it, among the compiled rules and the user theme's. Empty where none does, and for any surface but a window. Linear in the number of rules.

   .. cpp:function:: Resolution resolve(const PartFacts& facts) const noexcept

      The cascade for one part: the winning primitive declaration, then each of that primitive's parameters from the winning declaration of it among the matching rules that select the same primitive, else its declared default; and each material field from its winning declaration among all the matching rules. Linear in the number of rules; tables are small, and a theme with no rules returns at once.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:class:: ControlRoleTable final

   ``#include <gui_forms/theme/types/theme_types.hpp>``

   The recipes of every control visual role, indexed by ControlVisualRole, held out of line. They are most of a theme's description (one family of four state tables per role, some 100 KB), so holding them on the heap keeps a ThemeDefinition small enough to be an ordinary value. The table always holds exactly one family per role: it is made with default recipes, and a copy copies every recipe. Moving copies too (no move operations are declared), so a moved-from table still holds its recipes rather than none.


   .. rubric:: Construction

   .. cpp:function:: ControlRoleTable()

      Allocates the families, value-initialized in place on the heap.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: ControlStripePattern final

   ``#include <gui_forms/theme/types/theme_types.hpp>``

   A part's metrics and shape as a theme sets them (RecipeField::block_size to edge_shadow_colour), each unset where no rule sets it: the control then keeps its own metric and the part its rectangle. Lengths are logical units; a taper, flare or edge shadow of zero draws nothing. A part's stripe pattern (RecipeField::stripe_pitch to stripe_reduced_motion; the barber pole, owner rulings of 2026-11-11), each unset where no rule sets it, when the stripe effect keeps its own value: pitch auto (the fill's inner thickness, at least 8), a pale share of 0.4, 45 degrees, a backward lean travelling forward at 0.8 pitches a second, the fill as the theme paints it under translucent pale bands, a gloss over opaque stripe colours, and still bands under reduced motion. A pitch of 0 is ``auto``.

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<double> pitch

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<double> ratio

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<double> angle_degrees

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<StripeSense> lean

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<StripeSense> direction

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<double> speed

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<Color> colour

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<Color> ground

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<StripeGloss> gloss

   .. cpp:member:: std::optional<StripeReducedMotion> reduced_motion

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: ControlTextShadow final

   ``#include <gui_forms/theme/types/theme_types.hpp>``

   A text shadow (E9.6, CSS ``text-shadow``): the text's glyphs, moved by ``offset`` logical units (each axis within ±maximum_text_shadow_offset), blurred by ``blur_radius`` (the shadows' blur-radius convention, 0 to maximum_text_shadow_blur; both limits are the theme rules') and drawn in ``color`` under the glyphs. Data only in T2: the theme carries it, and the text stage (M4/M9) draws it, with a first-party blur so that it is the same on every host.

   .. cpp:member:: Point offset

   .. cpp:member:: double blur_radius

   .. cpp:member:: Color color

      Default: ``{Color::rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)}``.

   .. cpp:member:: double stroke_width

      The dial parts and the scrim (row 2.5; owner option (a)): an arc's thickness and a needle's width at its hub, in logical units (0 to maximum_recipe_stroke_width); the needle's outline; and the colour, its alpha the opacity, a scrim covers what lies behind with. Default: ``{4.0}``.

   .. cpp:member:: NeedleShape needle_shape

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:class:: EffectCanvas final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/effect_canvas/effect_canvas.hpp>``


   .. rubric:: Construction

   .. cpp:function:: EffectCanvas(Painter& painter, Size part, Insets outsets, double scale, std::uint32_t per_device_pixel, PartCornerRadii corners = {}, const std::shared_ptr<const PartRaster>* backdrop = nullptr) noexcept

      A canvas over ``painter`` for a part of ``part`` logical size whose origin is the painter's current origin, with ``outsets`` around it, at display ``scale``, and a budget of ``per_device_pixel`` operations for each device pixel of the part and its outsets. The painter must already be clipped to the part and its outsets and translated to the part's origin (the host does both); the canvas leaves its save depth as it found it. ``corners`` are the part's shape's corner radii (clip_part_shape). ``backdrop``, borrowed for the canvas's life, is the blurred backplane under the part and its outsets, one raster pixel per device pixel (draw_backdrop); null, or pointing at null, when there is none.


   .. rubric:: Properties

   .. cpp:function:: const EffectCanvasMeter& meter() const noexcept

      For the host: what the frame charged and refused.


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_rect(Rect rect, Color color) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_rounded_rect(Rect rect, double radius, Color color) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_circle(Point center, double radius, Color color) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void stroke_rect(Rect rect, Color color, double width) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void stroke_circle(Point center, double radius, Color color, double width) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void stroke_line(Point from, Point to, Color color, double width) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_linear_gradient(Rect rect, Point start, Point end, const EffectGradient& gradient, bool repeat) noexcept

      A linear gradient over ``rect`` from ``start`` to ``end``; ``repeat`` repeats it past them instead of extending its end colours.

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_radial_gradient(Rect rect, Point center, Size radii, const EffectGradient& gradient) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void soft_dot(Point center, double radius, Color color) noexcept

      A soft round dot: ``color`` at its centre fading to transparent at ``radius`` (PlaySuite's mist puffs and glints, drawn source-over).

   .. cpp:function:: void streak(Point head, Point tail, double width, Color color) noexcept

      A streak: a line of ``width`` from ``tail`` to ``head`` with a soft dot at the head (PlaySuite's particle streaks, simplified).

   .. cpp:function:: void stroke_polyline(const EffectPoints& points, const StrokeStyle& style) noexcept

      Version 2. Each batched call's fixed cost covers maximum_effect_batch elements; its pixels are charged on the batch's bounds, held to the area its marks can cover. An open polyline through 2 or more points with ``style``'s joins, caps and dashes (valid_stroke_style).

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_polygon(const EffectPoints& points, Color color, PolygonFillRule rule) noexcept

      A closed polygon through 3 or more points.

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_band(const EffectPoints& upper, const EffectPoints& lower, Color color) noexcept

      The area between two polylines of the same count, 2 or more.

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_rects(const EffectRects& rects, Color color) noexcept

      Each of 1 or more rectangles in one colour.

   .. cpp:function:: void stroke_segments(const EffectSegments& segments, const StrokeStyle& style) noexcept

      Each of 1 or more segments with ``style``'s caps and dashes, no joins.

   .. cpp:function:: void draw_markers(const EffectPoints& centers, const MarkerStyle& style) noexcept

      A marker centred on each of 1 or more points (valid_marker_style).

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_conic_gradient(Rect rect, Point center, double start_degrees, const EffectGradient& gradient) noexcept

      A conic (sweep) gradient over ``rect``: one turn clockwise from ``start_degrees`` about ``center`` (piece C9).

   .. cpp:function:: void fill_checker(Rect rect, const CheckerPattern& pattern) noexcept

      ``rect`` filled with a checkerboard (piece C7).

   .. cpp:function:: void save() noexcept

      Saves the clip and the offset; refused past maximum_effect_save_depth.

   .. cpp:function:: void restore() noexcept

      Restores the last save; a restore without a save does nothing.

   .. cpp:function:: void clip_rect(Rect rect) noexcept

      Narrows the clip to ``rect`` (in the current offset's coordinates).

   .. cpp:function:: void clip_rounded_rect(Rect rect, double radius) noexcept

      Narrows the clip to ``rect`` with every corner rounded by ``radius`` (clamped to half the shorter side; 0 is a plain clip_rect), in the current offset's coordinates: a fill's inner shape, inside its rim.

   .. cpp:function:: void translate(Point offset) noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void clip_part_shape() noexcept

      Narrows the clip to the part's own shape (EP-6): its box with the corners its host gives it (EffectInstance::paint), so an effect drawn over a rounded part stays inside its corners. It gives up the outsets until the next restore.

   .. cpp:function:: void draw_backdrop(Rect rect, double opacity) noexcept

      Draws the window's backplane under the part, blurred as the effect declares (EffectDeclaration::backdrop, EP-7), within ``rect`` (in the current coordinates) at ``opacity`` in [0, 1], device-aligned and never resampled. Refused, and counted, for an effect that does not declare a backdrop or a part whose host lends none (a part in the backplane plane, a headless paint, or a backplane that could not be made).

An effect varies their alpha for its own layers; it does not name colours of its own. Transparent black when the host supplies none.

.. cpp:struct:: EffectCost final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/effect/effect.hpp>``

   An effect's per-frame budget: ``fixed`` operations (proved by the plugin's static bound) plus ``per_device_pixel`` operations for each device pixel of the part and its outsets (metered by the canvas, which refuses drawing past it; owner decision 1(a)).

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: EffectDeclaration final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/effect/effect.hpp>``

   What an effect is, as its class declares it in ``static constexpr EffectDeclaration declaration``.

   .. cpp:member:: std::string_view name

      Registered (snake_case) name, as themes and applications write it.

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint32_t engine_version

      The vocabulary version the effect draws with, 1 to current_effect_vocabulary_version. Default: ``{1U}``.

   .. cpp:member:: EffectTimeModel time_model

      Default: ``{EffectTimeModel::ambient}``.

   .. cpp:member:: EffectCost cost

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint32_t setup_cost

      setup's own budget, in operations; a one-off, never charged to a frame. Zero for an effect without setup.

   .. cpp:member:: bool setup_on_geometry

   .. cpp:member:: std::size_t state_budget

      The most bytes the effect's state may take; sizeof must not exceed it.

   .. cpp:member:: Insets outsets

      How far the effect draws outside its part, in logical units. The engine clips to them, damages them and composites them source-over in the parent's paint order.

   .. cpp:member:: EffectInputKind input

      Default: ``{EffectInputKind::none}``.

   .. cpp:member:: bool backdrop

      Whether the effect reads the window's backplane under its part and outsets (EP-7, EffectCanvas::draw_backdrop), and how far the blur of that backdrop reaches, in logical units, in [0, maximum_effect_backdrop_margin]: three box passes of half width floor(backdrop_margin * scale / 3) device pixels; 0 reads it unblurred.

   .. cpp:member:: double backdrop_margin

   .. cpp:member:: double frames_per_second

      Its ambient frame rate, at most the ambient cap (30). Default: ``{30.0}``.

.. cpp:struct:: EffectEntry final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/effect_registry/effect_registry.hpp>``

   One registered effect type: its declaration, its parent's name, its state layout and the thunks the engine calls. ``setup`` is null for an effect without one. ``verified`` is set at registration from the plugin's record.

.. cpp:namespace:: gui_forms

.. cpp:struct:: EffectFrame final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/effect/effect.hpp>``

   What one frame of ``run`` is given. ``ticks`` fixed ticks are due, the first of them numbered ``first_tick`` since the effect was set up; zero when the part repaints for another reason, so state advances once per due tick however often the part repaints. ``still`` is true while motion is paused (reduced motion, an occluded window, the motion switch): the effect draws its still pose and advances nothing. A random draw for tick t uses EffectRandom(seed, t), so draws do not depend on how ticks were grouped into frames.

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint32_t ticks

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t first_tick

   .. cpp:member:: double transition

   .. cpp:member:: bool still

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint64_t seed

      The part's logical size and the display scale (device pixels per logical unit).

   .. cpp:member:: double scale

      Default: ``{1.0}``.

   .. cpp:member:: std::array<EffectInputEvent, maximum_effect_input_events> input

   .. cpp:member:: std::uint32_t input_count

   .. cpp:member:: EffectColours colours

   .. cpp:member:: EffectPointer pointer

.. cpp:class:: EffectInstance final

   ``#include <gui_forms/engine/effect_registry/effect_registry.hpp>``


   .. rubric:: Construction

   .. cpp:function:: EffectInstance() noexcept = default


   .. rubric:: Properties

   .. cpp:function:: const EffectEntry* entry() const noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: EffectInputKind input_grant() const noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void set_input_grant(EffectInputKind grant) noexcept

      The input the hosting control grants the effect (design note section 6: none by default, set by the application through the control's effect_input property). Kept across attach and detach.

   .. cpp:function:: EffectPointer pointer() const noexcept

      The standing pointer state the next frame is given.

   .. cpp:function:: EffectColours colours() const noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: void set_colours(EffectColours colours) noexcept

      The colours the next frames are given (EffectFrame::colours); the host sets them before each paint. Kept across attach and detach.

   .. cpp:function:: std::uint64_t state_checksum() const noexcept

      The state's bytes, hashed (FNV-1a): the motion checksum.

   .. cpp:function:: const EffectInstanceCounters& counters() const noexcept

   .. cpp:function:: const EffectCanvasMeter& last_meter() const noexcept


   .. rubric:: Methods

   .. cpp:function:: void attach(const EffectEntry& entry, std::uint64_t seed, FrameTime now)

      Releases any effect held, then makes :cpp:func:`entry <gui_forms::EffectInstance::entry>`'s state, value- initialized, with ``seed``, its time starting at ``now``. On an allocation failure the instance is left empty and the exception propagates.

   .. cpp:function:: void detach() noexcept

      Releases the effect's state; the instance is empty.

   .. cpp:function:: EffectAdvance advance(FrameTime now) noexcept

      The engine clock reads ``now`` on an ambient frame: the ticks due since the last advance are added to the pending ones, at most maximum_effect_ticks_per_frame; the rest are skipped and counted.

   .. cpp:function:: void rebase(FrameTime now) noexcept

      Motion resumed after a pause: time restarts from ``now``, with no catch-up.

   .. cpp:function:: bool queue_input(const EffectInputEvent& event, EffectInputKind granted) noexcept

      Queues one input event for the next frame, if ``granted`` and the effect's declaration both admit its kind (pointer events for :cpp:func:`pointer <gui_forms::EffectInstance::pointer>`, keys too for ``pointer_and_keys``); otherwise it is ignored. Past maximum_effect_input_events queued, it is counted and dropped. Returns whether it was queued. Never fails.

   .. cpp:function:: bool note_pointer(const PointerEvent& event, Point part_origin) noexcept

      Takes one pointer event the hosting control received, at ``position`` in the control's coordinates, for a part whose origin there is ``part_origin``: converts it to part-local units, queues a move, press or release under the grant (queue_input), and updates the standing pointer state (EffectFrame::pointer) for every action, enter and leave setting ``inside``. Does nothing without an effect, a grant and an effect declaring pointer input. Returns whether the effect's frame changed (an event queued or the state moved), when the host repaints the effect's extent. Never fails.

   .. cpp:function:: template <typename Parent> void paint(Painter& painter, Rect part, double scale, bool still, const EffectView<Parent>& view, PartShape shape = PartShape{}, const Control* host = nullptr)

      Runs the effect's frame over ``painter``, whose origin is the parent's, for the part at ``part`` (logical, in the painter's coordinates) at display ``scale``. Runs setup first when due. ``still`` draws the still pose and consumes no ticks. ``view`` must be the effect's parent's view. ``shape`` is the part's shape, which EffectCanvas::clip_part_shape clips to; by default a plain rectangle. ``host`` is the control that paints the effect, through which a backdrop is sampled from its window (EP-7); null for a paint with no control, which lends no backdrop.
